public static double devsq(double[] v) { double r = Double.NaN; if (v != null && v.length >= 1) { double m = 0; double s = 0; int n = v.length; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { s += v[i]; } m = s / n; s = 0; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { s += (v[i] - m) * (v[i] - m); } r = (n == 1) ? 0 : s; } return r; }
